const WEEKDAYS = ["Sun","Mon","Tue","Wed","Thu","Fri","Sat"];


function monthsBetween(from, to) {

  const [fy,fm] = from.split("-").map(Number);

  const [ty,tm] = to.split("-").map(Number);

  const months = [];

  let y = fy, m = fm;

  while (y < ty || (y === ty && m <= tm)) {

    months.push(`${y}-${String(m).padStart(2,"0")}`);

    m++; if (m > 12) { m = 1; y++; }

    if (months.length > 240) break;

  }

  return months;

}

  tag: `<svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="var(--brass)" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12.6 2.6a2 2 0 0 0-1.4-.6H4a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v7.2a2 2 0 0 0 .6 1.4l8.7 8.7a2.4 2.4 0 0 0 3.4 0l6.6-6.6a2.4 2.4 0 0 0 0-3.4z"/><circle cx="7.5" cy="7.5" r="1.2" fill="var(--brass)"/></svg>`,

  home: `<svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="var(--brass)" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="m3 9 9-7 9 7v11a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2z"/><polyline points="9 22 9 12 15 12 15 22"/></svg>`,

  clock: `<svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><polyline points="12 6 12 12 16 14"/></svg>`,

  download: `<svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M21 15v4a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-4"/><polyline points="7 10 12 15 17 10"/><line x1="12" y1="15" x2="12" y2="3"/></svg>`,

};

const PAYMENT_METHODS = ["Cash", "Bank Transfer", "Credit/Debit Card", "PayMe/FPS", "Other"];

const ROOM_OPTIONS = ["Room A", "Room B", "Room C"];


const emptyData = { teachers: [], students: [], lessonTypes: [], attendance: [], roomRentals: [], payments: [] };

let data = { ...emptyData };

let ui = {

  tab: "dashboard",

  dashMonth: monthKeyOf(todayISO()),

  attStudent: "",

  attMonth: monthKeyOf(todayISO()),

  attSelectedDate: null,

  attEditingId: null,

  attView: "calendar",

  payAmount: "",

  payStudentSel: "",

  payMonthSel: monthKeyOf(todayISO()),

  teacherForm: { editingId: null, name: "", phone: "", instrument: "" },

  studentForm: { editingId: null, name: "", studentType: "Regular", enrollments: [{ lessonTypeId: "", teacherId: "", weekday: "" }] },

  lessonTypeForm: { editingId: null, name: "", durationMins: "", price: "", teacherSharePercent: 60 },

  roomRentalForm: { editingId: null, teacherId: "", date: todayISO(), minutes: "", remark: "", room: ROOM_OPTIONS[0], rate: ROOM_RATE_PER_MIN },

  reportFrom: monthKeyOf(todayISO()),

  reportTo: monthKeyOf(todayISO()),

};

const WEEKDAY_NAMES = ["Sunday","Monday","Tuesday","Wednesday","Thursday","Friday","Saturday"];


let storageOK = true;

let lastSaveError = "";


async function loadData() {

  if (typeof window.storage === "undefined") {

    storageOK = false;

  } else {

    try {

      const res = await window.storage.get("centre-data", false);

      if (res && res.value) data = { ...emptyData, ...JSON.parse(res.value) };

    } catch (e) { /* nothing saved yet — this is normal on first run */ }

  }


  // migrate lesson types

  if (!data.lessonTypes || data.lessonTypes.length === 0) {

    data.lessonTypes = DEFAULT_LESSON_TYPES.map((lt) => ({ ...lt, id: uid() }));

  } else {

    data.lessonTypes = data.lessonTypes.map((l) => ({ ...l, teacherSharePercent: l.teacherSharePercent != null ? l.teacherSharePercent : DEFAULT_SHARE_PERCENT }));

    if (!data.lessonTypes.some((l) => l.name === "Piano Accompaniment")) {

      data.lessonTypes.push({ id: uid(), name: "Piano Accompaniment", durationMins: 30, price: 300, teacherSharePercent: 60 });

    }

  }


  // migrate students -> enrollments model

  data.students = (data.students || []).map((s) => {

    if (!s.enrollments) {

      const enrollments = [];

      if (s.lessonTypeId) enrollments.push({ id: uid(), lessonTypeId: s.lessonTypeId, teacherId: s.teacherId || "" });

      return { id: s.id, name: s.name, studentType: s.studentType || "Regular", enrollments };

    }

    return { ...s, studentType: s.studentType || "Regular" };

  });


  // migrate attendance -> drop legacy boolean records, ensure teacherId present

  data.attendance = (data.attendance || []).filter((a) => a.status).map((a) => {

    if (a.teacherId) return a;

    const st = data.students.find((s) => s.id === a.studentId);

    const enroll = st && st.enrollments && st.enrollments.find((e) => e.lessonTypeId === a.lessonTypeId);

    const fallback = st && st.enrollments && st.enrollments[0];

    return { ...a, teacherId: enroll ? enroll.teacherId : (fallback ? fallback.teacherId : "") };

  });


  await saveData();

}

async function saveData() {

  if (typeof window.storage === "undefined") { storageOK = false; return; }

  try {

    await window.storage.set("centre-data", JSON.stringify(data), false);

    storageOK = true; lastSaveError = "";

  } catch (e) {

    storageOK = false;

    lastSaveError = (e && e.message) ? e.message : String(e);

    console.error("Save failed", e);

  }

}


function staffSVG(dots, height) {

  height = height || 34;

  const lines = [6, 12, 17, 22, 28];

  let s = `<svg class="staff" viewBox="0 0 400 34" width="100%" height="${height}" preserveAspectRatio="none">`;

  lines.forEach((y) => (s += `<line x1="0" y1="${y}" x2="400" y2="${y}" stroke="var(--line)" stroke-width="1"/>`));

  (dots || []).forEach((d) => (s += `<circle cx="${d.x}" cy="${d.y}" r="4.5" fill="${d.color || 'var(--brass)'}"/>`));

  s += `</svg>`;

  return s;

}


// ---------- derived helpers ----------

function teacherName(id) { const t = data.teachers.find((t) => t.id === id); return t ? t.name : "Unassigned"; }

function studentName(id) { const s = data.students.find((s) => s.id === id); return s ? s.name : "Unknown"; }

function lessonType(id) { return data.lessonTypes.find((l) => l.id === id); }

function lessonTypeLabel(lt) { return lt ? `${lt.name} · ${lt.durationMins}min · ${money(lt.price)}` : "Not set"; }


function sessionMinutes(rec) {

  const lt = lessonType(rec.lessonTypeId);

  if (!lt) return 0;

  if (rec.status === "leave") return 0;

  if (rec.status === "makeup") return lt.durationMins + Number(rec.extraMinutes || 0);

  return lt.durationMins;

}

function sessionPrice(rec) {

  const lt = lessonType(rec.lessonTypeId);

  if (!lt) return 0;

  const perMin = lt.price / lt.durationMins;

  return sessionMinutes(rec) * perMin;

}

function sessionSharePercent(rec) {

  const lt = lessonType(rec.lessonTypeId);

  return rec.sharePercent != null ? rec.sharePercent : (lt && lt.teacherSharePercent != null ? lt.teacherSharePercent : DEFAULT_SHARE_PERCENT);

}

function sessionTeacherShare(rec) { return sessionPrice(rec) * (sessionSharePercent(rec) / 100); }


function studentMonthTotal(studentId, month) {

  return data.attendance.filter((a) => a.studentId === studentId && a.date.slice(0,7) === month).reduce((s,a) => s + sessionPrice(a), 0);

}

function studentMonthBreakdown(studentId, month) {

  const recs = data.attendance.filter((a) => a.studentId === studentId && a.date.slice(0,7) === month && a.status !== "leave");

  const byType = {};

  recs.forEach((r) => { const lt = lessonType(r.lessonTypeId); const key = lt ? lt.name : "Other"; byType[key] = (byType[key]||0) + sessionPrice(r); });

  const total = Object.values(byType).reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);

  return { byType, total };

}

function studentAllTimeCharged(studentId) { return data.attendance.filter((a) => a.studentId === studentId).reduce((s,a) => s + sessionPrice(a), 0); }

function studentAllTimePaid(studentId) { return data.payments.filter((p) => p.studentId === studentId).reduce((s,p) => s + Number(p.amount), 0); }


function roomRentalFee(r) { return Number(r.minutes) * (r.rate != null ? Number(r.rate) : ROOM_RATE_PER_MIN); }

function teacherMonthRoomFee(teacherId, month) {

  return data.roomRentals.filter((r) => r.teacherId === teacherId && r.date.slice(0,7) === month).reduce((s,r) => s + roomRentalFee(r), 0);

}

function teacherMonthBreakdown(teacherId, month) {

  const recs = data.attendance.filter((a) => a.teacherId === teacherId && a.date.slice(0,7) === month);

  const income = recs.reduce((s,r) => s + sessionPrice(r), 0);

  const gross = recs.reduce((s,r) => s + sessionTeacherShare(r), 0);

  const rental = teacherMonthRoomFee(teacherId, month);

  const net = gross - rental;

  const centre = income - net;

  return { income, gross, rental, net, centre };

}


// ---------- teacher form actions ----------

function resetTeacherForm() { ui.teacherForm = { editingId: null, name: "", phone: "", instrument: "" }; }

function editTeacher(id) { const t = data.teachers.find((t) => t.id === id); if (!t) return; ui.teacherForm = { editingId: id, name: t.name, phone: t.phone || "", instrument: t.instrument || "" }; render(); }

function cancelTeacherForm() { resetTeacherForm(); render(); }

async function saveTeacherForm() {

  const name = document.getElementById("teName").value.trim();

  const phone = document.getElementById("tePhone").value;

  const instrument = document.getElementById("teInstrument").value;

  if (!name) return;

  if (ui.teacherForm.editingId) {

    const t = data.teachers.find((t) => t.id === ui.teacherForm.editingId);

    if (t) Object.assign(t, { name, phone, instrument });

  } else {

    data.teachers.push({ id: uid(), name, phone, instrument });

  }

  resetTeacherForm(); await saveData(); render();

}

async function removeTeacher(id) { data.teachers = data.teachers.filter((t) => t.id !== id); if (ui.teacherForm.editingId===id) resetTeacherForm(); await saveData(); render(); }


// ---------- student form actions ----------

function resetStudentForm() { ui.studentForm = { editingId: null, name: "", studentType: "Regular", enrollments: [{ lessonTypeId: "", teacherId: "", weekday: "" }] }; }

function editStudent(id) {

  const s = data.students.find((s) => s.id === id); if (!s) return;

  ui.studentForm = {

    editingId: id, name: s.name, studentType: s.studentType || "Regular",

    enrollments: (s.enrollments && s.enrollments.length) ? s.enrollments.map((e) => ({ lessonTypeId: e.lessonTypeId||"", teacherId: e.teacherId||"", weekday: e.weekday!=null?String(e.weekday):"" })) : [{ lessonTypeId: "", teacherId: "", weekday: "" }],

  };

  render();

}

function cancelStudentForm() { resetStudentForm(); render(); }

function syncStudentFormFromDOM() {

  const nameEl = document.getElementById("stName"); if (!nameEl) return;

  ui.studentForm.name = nameEl.value;

  ui.studentForm.studentType = document.getElementById("stType").value;

  ui.studentForm.enrollments = ui.studentForm.enrollments.map((en, i) => ({

    lessonTypeId: document.getElementById("stEnrollLT"+i) ? document.getElementById("stEnrollLT"+i).value : en.lessonTypeId,

    teacherId: document.getElementById("stEnrollTeacher"+i) ? document.getElementById("stEnrollTeacher"+i).value : en.teacherId,

    weekday: document.getElementById("stEnrollWeekday"+i) ? document.getElementById("stEnrollWeekday"+i).value : en.weekday,

  }));

}

function addEnrollRow() { syncStudentFormFromDOM(); ui.studentForm.enrollments.push({ lessonTypeId: "", teacherId: "", weekday: "" }); render(); }

function removeEnrollRow(idx) {

  syncStudentFormFromDOM();

  ui.studentForm.enrollments.splice(idx, 1);

  if (!ui.studentForm.enrollments.length) ui.studentForm.enrollments.push({ lessonTypeId: "", teacherId: "", weekday: "" });

  render();

}

async function saveStudentForm() {

  syncStudentFormFromDOM();

  const name = ui.studentForm.name.trim();

  if (!name) return;

  const enrollments = ui.studentForm.enrollments.filter((e) => e.lessonTypeId).map((e) => ({ id: uid(), lessonTypeId: e.lessonTypeId, teacherId: e.teacherId, weekday: e.weekday === "" ? null : Number(e.weekday) }));

  if (ui.studentForm.editingId) {

    const s = data.students.find((s) => s.id === ui.studentForm.editingId);

    if (s) Object.assign(s, { name, studentType: ui.studentForm.studentType, enrollments });

  } else {

    data.students.push({ id: uid(), name, studentType: ui.studentForm.studentType, enrollments });

  }

  resetStudentForm(); await saveData(); render();

}

async function removeStudent(id) { data.students = data.students.filter((s) => s.id !== id); if (ui.studentForm.editingId===id) resetStudentForm(); await saveData(); render(); }

}

// ---------- attendance / session actions ----------

async function saveSessionFromForm(dateStr) {

  const lessonTypeId = document.getElementById("sessionLessonType").value;

  const teacherId = document.getElementById("sessionTeacher").value;

  const status = document.getElementById("sessionStatus").value;

  const extraMinutes = document.getElementById("sessionExtra").value;

  const shareVal = document.getElementById("sessionShare").value;

  const remark = document.getElementById("sessionRemark").value;

  const resolved = document.getElementById("sessionResolved").checked;

  const sharePercent = shareVal === "" ? null : Number(shareVal);

  if (ui.attEditingId === "new") {

    data.attendance.push({ id: uid(), studentId: ui.attStudent, date: dateStr, lessonTypeId, teacherId, status, extraMinutes: Number(extraMinutes||0), sharePercent, remark, resolved });

  } else {

    const rec = data.attendance.find((a) => a.id === ui.attEditingId);

    if (rec) Object.assign(rec, { lessonTypeId, teacherId, status, extraMinutes: Number(extraMinutes||0), sharePercent, remark, resolved });

  }

  ui.attEditingId = null;

  await saveData(); render();

}

async function deleteAttendanceRecord(id) { data.attendance = data.attendance.filter((a) => a.id !== id); if (ui.attEditingId===id) ui.attEditingId=null; await saveData(); render(); }

async function toggleResolved(id) { const rec = data.attendance.find((a) => a.id === id); if (rec) rec.resolved = !rec.resolved; await saveData(); render(); }

async function updateRemark(id, remark) { const rec = data.attendance.find((a) => a.id === id); if (rec) rec.remark = remark; await saveData(); }


function resetRoomRentalForm() { ui.roomRentalForm = { editingId: null, teacherId: "", date: todayISO(), minutes: "", remark: "", room: ROOM_OPTIONS[0], rate: ROOM_RATE_PER_MIN }; }

function editRoomRental(id) {

  const r = data.roomRentals.find((r) => r.id === id); if (!r) return;

  render();

}

  const minutes = Number(document.getElementById("rrMinutes").value);

  const remark = document.getElementById("rrRemark").value;

  if (!studentId || !amount) return;

  await saveData(); render();

}

async function removePayment(id) { data.payments = data.payments.filter((p) => p.id !== id); await saveData(); render(); }


function fileToBase64(file) {

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {

    const reader = new FileReader();

    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result);

    reader.onerror = reject;

    reader.readAsDataURL(file);

  });

}


// ---------- export ----------

function exportExcel(fromMonth, toMonth) {

  if (typeof XLSX === "undefined") { alert("Excel export library did not load — check your internet connection."); return; }

  const months = monthsBetween(fromMonth, toMonth);

  const summaryRows = months.map((mo) => {

    let income=0, net=0, rental=0;

    data.teachers.forEach((t) => { const b = teacherMonthBreakdown(t.id, mo); income+=b.income; net+=b.net; rental+=b.rental; });

    return { Month: monthLabel(mo), "Total Session Income": Number(income.toFixed(2)), "Teacher Payouts (Net)": Number(net.toFixed(2)), "Room Rental Income": Number(rental.toFixed(2)), "Centre Net Income": Number((income-net).toFixed(2)) };

  });

  const attRows = data.attendance.filter((a) => months.includes(a.date.slice(0,7))).sort((a,b)=>a.date<b.date?-1:1).map((a) => ({

    Date: a.date, Student: studentName(a.studentId), Teacher: teacherName(a.teacherId), "Lesson Type": (lessonType(a.lessonTypeId)||{}).name || "",

    Status: a.status, Minutes: sessionMinutes(a), Price: Number(sessionPrice(a).toFixed(2)), "Teacher Share %": sessionSharePercent(a),

    "Teacher Amount": Number(sessionTeacherShare(a).toFixed(2)), Remark: a.remark || "",

  }));

  const payRows = data.payments.filter((p) => months.includes(p.month)).sort((a,b)=>a.date<b.date?-1:1).map((p) => ({

    Date: p.date, Student: studentName(p.studentId), Month: monthLabel(p.month), Amount: Number(Number(p.amount).toFixed(2)), Method: p.method || "",

  }));

  const rentRows = data.roomRentals.filter((r) => months.includes(r.date.slice(0,7))).sort((a,b)=>a.date<b.date?-1:1).map((r) => ({

    Date: r.date, Room: r.room || ROOM_OPTIONS[0], Teacher: teacherName(r.teacherId), Minutes: r.minutes, Rate: r.rate!=null?r.rate:ROOM_RATE_PER_MIN, Fee: Number(roomRentalFee(r).toFixed(2)), Remark: r.remark || "",

  }));

  const balanceRows = data.students.map((s) => ({ Student: s.name, "Total Charged": Number(studentAllTimeCharged(s.id).toFixed(2)), "Total Paid": Number(studentAllTimePaid(s.id).toFixed(2)), Balance: Number((studentAllTimeCharged(s.id)-studentAllTimePaid(s.id)).toFixed(2)) }));


  const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();

  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(summaryRows), "Summary");

  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(attRows), "Attendance");

  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(payRows), "Payments");

  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rentRows), "Room Rentals");

  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(balanceRows), "Student Balances");

  XLSX.writeFile(wb, `HK-Elite-Strings_${fromMonth}_to_${toMonth}.xlsx`);

}


function drawPdfTable(doc, startY, headers, rows, colWidths) {

  const pageHeight = doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight();

  const x0 = 40;

  let y = startY;

  function drawHeader() {

    doc.setFont("helvetica","bold"); doc.setFontSize(9);

    let x = x0;

    headers.forEach((h,i) => { doc.text(String(h), x, y); x += colWidths[i]; });

    y += 6;

    doc.setDrawColor(180);

    doc.line(x0, y, x0 + colWidths.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0), y);

    y += 12;

    doc.setFont("helvetica","normal"); doc.setFontSize(9);

  }

  drawHeader();

  rows.forEach((row) => {

    if (y > pageHeight - 60) { doc.addPage(); y = 50; drawHeader(); }

    let x = x0;

    row.forEach((cell,i) => { doc.text(String(cell), x, y); x += colWidths[i]; });

    y += 16;

  });

  return y;

}


function exportPDF(fromMonth, toMonth) {

  if (typeof window.jspdf === "undefined") { alert("PDF export library did not load — check your internet connection."); return; }

  const { jsPDF } = window.jspdf;

  const doc = new jsPDF({ unit: "pt", format: "a4" });

  let y = 44;

  try { doc.addImage(LOGO_DATA_URL, "PNG", 40, y, 46, 42); } catch (e) {}

  doc.setFont("helvetica","bold"); doc.setFontSize(16);

  doc.text(COMPANY_NAME, 96, y + 18);

  doc.setFont("helvetica","normal"); doc.setFontSize(10);

  doc.text("Income & Expenditure Report", 96, y + 34);

  doc.text(`Period: ${monthLabel(fromMonth)} to ${monthLabel(toMonth)}`, 96, y + 48);

  doc.text(`Generated: ${todayISO()}`, 96, y + 62);

  y += 100;


  const months = monthsBetween(fromMonth, toMonth);

  let totalIncome = 0, totalNet = 0, totalRental = 0;

  const summaryRows = months.map((mo) => {

    let income=0, net=0, rental=0;

    data.teachers.forEach((t) => { const b = teacherMonthBreakdown(t.id, mo); income+=b.income; net+=b.net; rental+=b.rental; });

    totalIncome += income; totalNet += net; totalRental += rental;

    return [monthLabel(mo), money(income), money(net), money(rental), money(income-net)];

  });

  y = drawPdfTable(doc, y, ["Month","Session Income","Teacher Payouts","Room Rental","Centre Net Income"], summaryRows, [95,110,105,90,115]);


  y += 24;

  doc.setFont("helvetica","bold"); doc.setFontSize(11);

  doc.text("Totals for period", 40, y); y += 18;

  doc.setFont("helvetica","normal"); doc.setFontSize(10);

  doc.text(`Total Session Income: ${money(totalIncome)}`, 40, y); y += 15;

  doc.text(`Total Teacher Payouts: ${money(totalNet)}`, 40, y); y += 15;

  doc.text(`Total Room Rental Income: ${money(totalRental)}`, 40, y); y += 15;

  doc.setFont("helvetica","bold");

  doc.text(`Centre Net Income: ${money(totalIncome-totalNet)}`, 40, y); y += 26;


  const payRows = data.payments.filter((p) => months.includes(p.month)).sort((a,b)=>a.date<b.date?-1:1)

    .map((p) => [p.date, studentName(p.studentId), monthLabel(p.month), p.method||"", money(p.amount)]);

  if (payRows.length) {

    if (y > doc.internal.pageSize.getHeight() - 100) { doc.addPage(); y = 50; }

    doc.setFont("helvetica","bold"); doc.setFontSize(11); doc.text("Payments received", 40, y); y += 18;

    y = drawPdfTable(doc, y, ["Date","Student","Month","Method","Amount"], payRows, [70,120,95,100,100]);

  }


  doc.save(`HK-Elite-Strings_IRD-Report_${fromMonth}_to_${toMonth}.pdf`);

}

function fieldHTML(label, innerHTML) { return `<label class="field"><span>${esc(label)}</span>${innerHTML}</label>`; }

function renderDashboard() {

  const month = ui.dashMonth;

  const rows = data.teachers.map((t) => ({ teacher: t, ...teacherMonthBreakdown(t.id, month) }));

  const totalIncome = rows.reduce((a,r) => a+r.income, 0);

  const totalNet = rows.reduce((a,r) => a+r.net, 0);

  const totalCentre = rows.reduce((a,r) => a+r.centre, 0);

  const totalRental = rows.reduce((a,r) => a+r.rental, 0);

  const dueCount = data.attendance.filter((a) => a.status === "leave" && !a.resolved).length;


  return `

    <div class="row">${fieldHTML("Month", `<input type="month" id="dashMonth" value="${month}"/>`)}</div>

    <div class="stats">

      <div class="statCard"><div class="statLabel">Session income — ${monthLabel(month)}</div><div class="statValue">${money(totalIncome)}</div></div>

      <div class="statCard"><div class="statLabel">Teacher net payouts</div><div class="statValue" style="color:var(--burgundy)">${money(totalNet)}</div></div>

      <div class="statCard"><div class="statLabel">Centre total</div><div class="statValue" style="color:var(--ledger)">${money(totalCentre)}</div></div>

      <div class="statCard"><div class="statLabel">Room rental collected</div><div class="statValue">${money(totalRental)}</div></div>

    </div>

    ${dueCount>0 ? `<div class="section" style="border-color:var(--burgundy)"><div class="sectionTitle">${ICONS.clock}<h2 style="color:var(--burgundy)">${dueCount} lesson${dueCount===1?"":"s"} due for make-up</h2></div><p class="muted" style="margin:6px 0 0">See "Lessons Due" under Attendance to schedule make-ups.</p></div>` : ""}

    <div class="section">

      <div class="sectionHead"><div class="sectionTitle">${ICONS.wallet}<h2>Payout by teacher</h2></div></div>

      ${rows.length===0 ? `<p class="muted">Add a teacher to see payouts here.</p>` : `

        <table>

          <thead><tr><th>Teacher</th><th class="right">Session income</th><th class="right">Gross share</th><th class="right">Room rental</th><th class="right">Net payout</th><th class="right">Centre keeps</th></tr></thead>

          <tbody>

            ${rows.map((r) => `

              <tr>

                <td>${esc(r.teacher.name)}</td>

                <td class="right mono">${money(r.income)}</td>

                <td class="right mono">${money(r.gross)}</td>

                <td class="right mono" style="color:var(--burgundy)">${r.rental>0?"-"+money(r.rental):money(0)}</td>

                <td class="right mono" style="color:var(--ledger);font-weight:600">${money(r.net)}</td>

                <td class="right mono">${money(r.centre)}</td>

              </tr>`).join("")}

          </tbody>

        </table>

      `}

    </div>

  `;

}

function calendarMatrix(year, monthIndex) {

  const firstDay = new Date(year, monthIndex, 1);

  const startWeekday = firstDay.getDay();

  const daysInMonth = new Date(year, monthIndex + 1, 0).getDate();

  const cells = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < startWeekday; i++) cells.push(null);

  for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) cells.push(d);

  while (cells.length % 7 !== 0) cells.push(null);

  const weeks = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < cells.length; i += 7) weeks.push(cells.slice(i, i + 7));

  return weeks;

}


function calDayCellHTML(dateStr, dayNum) {

  const recs = data.attendance.filter((a) => a.studentId === ui.attStudent && a.date === dateStr);

  const selected = ui.attSelectedDate === dateStr;

  let inner = `<span class="calDayNum">${dayNum}</span>`;

  recs.slice(0,2).forEach((r) => {

    const label = r.status === "leave" ? "Leave" : `${sessionMinutes(r)}m`;

    inner += `<span class="calBadge ${r.status}">${esc(label)}</span>`;

  });

  if (recs.length > 2) inner += `<span class="muted" style="font-size:9px;">+${recs.length-2} more</span>`;

  return `<div class="calCell ${selected?"selected":""}" data-action="pickDay" data-date="${dateStr}">${inner}</div>`;

}


function renderAttendance() {

  const toggle = `

    <div class="viewToggle">

      <button class="viewToggleBtn ${ui.attView==="calendar"?"active":""}" data-action="setAttView" data-view="calendar">Calendar</button>

      <button class="viewToggleBtn ${ui.attView==="due"?"active":""}" data-action="setAttView" data-view="due">Lessons Due</button>

    </div>

  `;

  return `<div class="section"><div class="sectionHead"><div class="sectionTitle">${ICONS.cal}<h2>Attendance</h2></div></div>${toggle}${ui.attView==="calendar"?renderAttendanceCalendar():renderLessonsDue()}</div>`;

}


function renderAttendanceCalendar() {

  if (data.students.length === 0) return `<p class="muted">Add a student first to record attendance.</p>`;

  if (!ui.attStudent) ui.attStudent = data.students[0].id;

  const [y, m] = ui.attMonth.split("-").map(Number);

  const weeks = calendarMatrix(y, m - 1);


  let grid = `<div class="calGrid">`;

  WEEKDAYS.forEach((w) => (grid += `<div class="calHead">${w}</div>`));

  weeks.forEach((week) => {

    week.forEach((d) => {

      if (!d) { grid += `<div class="calCell empty"></div>`; return; }

      const dateStr = `${ui.attMonth}-${String(d).padStart(2,"0")}`;

      grid += calDayCellHTML(dateStr, d);

    });

  });

  grid += `</div>`;


  const student = data.students.find((s) => s.id === ui.attStudent);

  const hasWeeklySchedule = student && student.enrollments && student.enrollments.some((e) => e.weekday != null && e.weekday !== "");


  return `

    <div class="row" style="margin-bottom:10px;">

      ${fieldHTML("Student", `<select id="attStudentSel">${data.students.map((s) => `<option value="${s.id}" ${s.id===ui.attStudent?"selected":""}>${esc(s.name)}</option>`).join("")}</select>`)}

    </div>

    <div class="calNav">

      <button class="navBtn" data-action="attMonthShift" data-dir="-1">‹ Prev</button>

      <div class="calMonthLabel">${monthLabel(ui.attMonth)}</div>

      <button class="navBtn" data-action="attMonthShift" data-dir="1">Next ›</button>

      ${hasWeeklySchedule ? `<button class="btnGhost" data-action="generateMonthLessons">${ICONS.plus} Generate this month's lessons</button>` : ""}

    </div>

    ${hasWeeklySchedule ? `<small class="hint" style="margin-bottom:10px;">Fills in a "Present" lesson for every scheduled weekday this month that doesn't already have an entry — so the charge automatically matches how many lessons actually fall in the month.</small>` : ""}

    ${grid}

    ${ui.attSelectedDate ? renderDayPanel(ui.attSelectedDate) : `<p class="muted">Click a day to add or edit lessons for that date.</p>`}

  `;

}


async function generateMonthLessons() {

  const student = data.students.find((s) => s.id === ui.attStudent);

  if (!student || !student.enrollments) return;

  const [y, m] = ui.attMonth.split("-").map(Number);

  const daysInMonth = new Date(y, m, 0).getDate();

  let added = 0;

  student.enrollments.forEach((en) => {

    if (en.weekday == null || en.weekday === "") return;

    const wd = Number(en.weekday);

    for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {

      const dateObj = new Date(y, m - 1, d);

      if (dateObj.getDay() !== wd) continue;

      const dateStr = `${ui.attMonth}-${String(d).padStart(2,"0")}`;

      const exists = data.attendance.some((a) => a.studentId === ui.attStudent && a.date === dateStr && a.lessonTypeId === en.lessonTypeId);

      if (exists) continue;

      data.attendance.push({

        id: uid(), studentId: ui.attStudent, date: dateStr, lessonTypeId: en.lessonTypeId, teacherId: en.teacherId,

        status: "present", extraMinutes: 0, sharePercent: null, remark: "", resolved: false,

      });

      added++;

    }

  });

  await saveData(); render();

  if (added === 0) alert("No new lessons to add — this month's scheduled dates are already filled in.");

}


function renderDayPanel(dateStr) {

  const recs = data.attendance.filter((a) => a.studentId === ui.attStudent && a.date === dateStr);

  let html = `<div class="editPanel"><div class="sectionTitle" style="margin-bottom:10px;"><h2 style="font-size:15px;">${esc(studentName(ui.attStudent))} — ${dateStr}</h2></div>`;

  if (recs.length) {

    html += recs.map((r) => {

      const lt = lessonType(r.lessonTypeId);

      const pct = sessionSharePercent(r);

      return `

      <div class="listRow">

        <div>

          <span class="calBadge ${r.status}">${r.status}</span>

          <span style="font-weight:600;margin-left:6px;">${esc(lt?lt.name:"—")}</span>

          <span class="muted"> — ${esc(teacherName(r.teacherId))}, ${sessionMinutes(r)} min</span>

          <div class="muted" style="font-size:12px;">${money(sessionPrice(r))} total · teacher ${pct}% = ${money(sessionTeacherShare(r))}${r.remark?` · "${esc(r.remark)}"`:""}</div>

        </div>

        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;">

          <button class="btnGhost" data-action="editSession" data-id="${r.id}">Edit</button>

          <button class="iconBtn" data-action="deleteSession" data-id="${r.id}">${ICONS.trash}</button>

        </div>

      </div>`;

    }).join("");

  } else {

    html += `<p class="muted">No lessons logged this day yet.</p>`;

  }

  if (ui.attEditingId) {

    html += renderSessionForm();

  } else {

    html += `<div class="row" style="margin-top:10px;"><button class="btnPrimary" data-action="addSession">${ICONS.plus} Add lesson on this day</button><button class="btnGhost" data-action="closeDayPanel">Close</button></div>`;

  }

  html += `</div>`;

  return html;

}


function renderSessionForm() {

  const editing = ui.attEditingId !== "new";

  const rec = editing ? data.attendance.find((a) => a.id === ui.attEditingId) : null;

  const student = data.students.find((s) => s.id === ui.attStudent);

  const defaultEnroll = student && student.enrollments && student.enrollments[0];

  const ltId = rec ? rec.lessonTypeId : (defaultEnroll ? defaultEnroll.lessonTypeId : "");

  const teacherId = rec ? rec.teacherId : (defaultEnroll ? defaultEnroll.teacherId : "");

  const status = rec ? rec.status : "present";

  const extra = rec ? rec.extraMinutes : 0;

  const remark = rec ? (rec.remark||"") : "";

  const resolved = rec ? !!rec.resolved : false;

  const sharePct = rec ? sessionSharePercent(rec) : (lessonType(ltId) ? lessonType(ltId).teacherSharePercent : 60);


  return `

    <div class="formCard" style="margin-top:10px;">

      <div class="row">

        ${fieldHTML("Lesson type", `<select id="sessionLessonType">${data.lessonTypes.map((l) => `<option value="${l.id}" ${l.id===ltId?"selected":""}>${esc(lessonTypeLabel(l))}</option>`).join("")}</select>`)}

        ${fieldHTML("Teacher", `<select id="sessionTeacher"><option value="">Unassigned</option>${data.teachers.map((t) => `<option value="${t.id}" ${t.id===teacherId?"selected":""}>${esc(t.name)}</option>`).join("")}</select>`)}

        ${fieldHTML("Status", `

          <select id="sessionStatus">

            <option value="present" ${status==="present"?"selected":""}>Present</option>

            <option value="leave" ${status==="leave"?"selected":""}>Leave (owes make-up)</option>

            <option value="makeup" ${status==="makeup"?"selected":""}>Make-up (extra minutes)</option>

          </select>`)}

      </div>

      <div class="row">

        ${fieldHTML("Extra make-up minutes", `<input id="sessionExtra" type="number" value="${extra||0}"/>`)}

        ${fieldHTML("Teacher share %", `<input id="sessionShare" type="number" min="0" max="100" value="${sharePct}"/>`)}

      </div>

      <div class="row">${fieldHTML("Remark", `<textarea id="sessionRemark" rows="2" placeholder="e.g. Leave on 3 Aug, make up 15 min here + 15 min next lesson">${esc(remark)}</textarea>`)}</div>

      <div class="row"><label class="checkboxRow"><input type="checkbox" id="sessionResolved" ${resolved?"checked":""}/> Make-up arranged / resolved</label></div>

      <div class="row">

        <button class="btnPrimary" data-action="saveSession">${ICONS.check} Save</button>

        <button class="btnGhost" data-action="cancelSession">Cancel</button>

      </div>

    </div>

  `;

}


function renderLessonsDue() {

  const all = data.attendance.filter((a) => a.status === "leave").sort((a,b) => a.date<b.date?1:-1);

  const outstanding = all.filter((a) => !a.resolved);

  const resolvedList = all.filter((a) => a.resolved);


  const itemHTML = (rec) => {

    const lt = lessonType(rec.lessonTypeId);

    return `

      <div class="dueItem">

        <div style="flex:1;min-width:220px;">

          <div><span style="font-weight:600">${esc(studentName(rec.studentId))}</span> <span class="muted">— left on ${rec.date}${lt?` (${lt.durationMins} min ${esc(lt.name)})`:""}, ${esc(teacherName(rec.teacherId))}</span></div>

          <textarea data-action="editDueRemark" data-id="${rec.id}" rows="2" style="margin-top:6px;">${esc(rec.remark||"")}</textarea>

        </div>

        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;align-items:flex-end;">

          <select data-action="changeDueStatus" data-id="${rec.id}" style="font-size:12px;">

            <option value="leave" selected>Still a leave</option>

            <option value="present">Change to Present</option>

            <option value="makeup">Change to Make-up</option>

          </select>

          ${rec.resolved

            ? `<button class="btnGhost" data-action="toggleResolved" data-id="${rec.id}">Reopen (undo)</button>`

            : `<button class="btnPrimary" data-action="toggleResolved" data-id="${rec.id}">${ICONS.check} Mark made up</button>`}

          <span class="muted" style="font-size:11px;">or edit fully via the Calendar view on ${rec.date}</span>

        </div>

      </div>

    `;

  };


  let html = `<h3 style="font-family:var(--font-display);font-weight:400;font-size:15px;margin:0 0 6px;">Outstanding (${outstanding.length})</h3>`;

  html += outstanding.length ? outstanding.map(itemHTML).join("") : `<p class="muted">No outstanding make-up lessons — everyone's caught up.</p>`;

  html += `<h3 style="font-family:var(--font-display);font-weight:400;font-size:15px;margin:22px 0 6px;">Resolved (${resolvedList.length})</h3>`;

  html += resolvedList.length ? resolvedList.map(itemHTML).join("") : `<p class="muted">None yet.</p>`;

  return html;

}

async function changeDueStatus(id, status) {

  const rec = data.attendance.find((a) => a.id === id);

  if (rec) rec.status = status;

  await saveData(); render();

}


function renderRoomRental() {

  const sorted = [...data.roomRentals].sort((a,b) => a.date<b.date?1:-1);

  const f = ui.roomRentalForm;

  return `

    <div class="section">

      <div class="sectionHead"><div class="sectionTitle">${ICONS.home}<h2>Room Rental</h2></div></div>

      <p class="muted" style="margin-top:-6px;">Deducted from that teacher's payout. Rate defaults to ${money(ROOM_RATE_PER_MIN)}/min but can be changed per entry below.</p>

      <div class="formCard">

        <div class="row">

          ${fieldHTML("Room", `<select id="rrRoom">${ROOM_OPTIONS.map((r) => `<option value="${r}" ${f.room===r?"selected":""}>${r}</option>`).join("")}</select>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("Teacher", `<select id="rrTeacher"><option value="">Select teacher</option>${data.teachers.map((t) => `<option value="${t.id}" ${f.teacherId===t.id?"selected":""}>${esc(t.name)}</option>`).join("")}</select>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("Date", `<input id="rrDate" type="date" value="${f.date||todayISO()}"/>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("Minutes", `<input id="rrMinutes" type="number" placeholder="60" value="${esc(f.minutes)}"/>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("Rate ($/min)", `<input id="rrRate" type="number" step="0.1" value="${esc(f.rate!=null?f.rate:ROOM_RATE_PER_MIN)}"/>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("Remark", `<input id="rrRemark" placeholder="Optional" value="${esc(f.remark)}"/>`)}

        </div>

        <div class="row">

          <button class="btnPrimary" data-action="saveRoomRentalForm">${ICONS.check} ${f.editingId?"Save changes":"Add"}</button>

          ${f.editingId?`<button class="btnGhost" data-action="cancelRoomRentalForm">Cancel</button>`:""}

        </div>

      </div>

      ${sorted.length===0 ? `<p class="muted">No room rentals recorded yet.</p>` : `

        <table>

          <thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Room</th><th>Teacher</th><th>Remark</th><th class="right">Minutes</th><th class="right">Rate</th><th class="right">Fee</th><th></th></tr></thead>

          <tbody>

            ${sorted.map((r) => `

              <tr>

                <td>${r.date}</td>

                <td>${esc(r.room||ROOM_OPTIONS[0])}</td>

                <td>${esc(teacherName(r.teacherId))}</td>

                <td class="muted">${esc(r.remark||"")}</td>

                <td class="right mono">${r.minutes}</td>

                <td class="right mono">${money(r.rate!=null?r.rate:ROOM_RATE_PER_MIN)}</td>

                <td class="right mono" style="color:var(--burgundy)">${money(roomRentalFee(r))}</td>

                <td class="right" style="display:flex;gap:8px;justify-content:flex-end;">

                  <button class="btnGhost" data-action="editRoomRental" data-id="${r.id}">Edit</button>

                  <button class="iconBtn" data-action="removeRoomRental" data-id="${r.id}">${ICONS.trash}</button>

                </td>

              </tr>`).join("")}

          </tbody>

        </table>

      `}

    </div>

  `;

}


function renderPayments() {

  const sorted = [...data.payments].sort((a,b) => a.date<b.date?1:-1);

  const breakdown = (ui.payStudentSel && ui.payMonthSel) ? studentMonthBreakdown(ui.payStudentSel, ui.payMonthSel) : null;

  return `

    <div class="section">

      <div class="sectionHead"><div class="sectionTitle">${ICONS.wallet}<h2>Payments</h2></div></div>

      <div class="formCard">

        <div class="row">

          ${fieldHTML("Student", `<select id="payStudent"><option value="">Select student</option>${data.students.map((s) => `<option value="${s.id}" ${s.id===ui.payStudentSel?"selected":""}>${esc(s.name)}</option>`).join("")}</select>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("For month", `<input type="month" id="payMonth" value="${ui.payMonthSel}"/>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("Amount ($)", `<input type="number" id="payAmount" value="${esc(ui.payAmount)}"/>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("Paid on", `<input type="date" id="payDate" value="${todayISO()}"/>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("Payment method", `<select id="payMethod">${PAYMENT_METHODS.map((m) => `<option value="${m}">${m}</option>`).join("")}</select>`)}

          ${fieldHTML("Receipt (optional)", `<input type="file" id="payReceipt" accept="image/*,.pdf"/>`)}

        </div>

        ${breakdown && Object.keys(breakdown.byType).length ? `<div class="breakdownBox">

          ${Object.entries(breakdown.byType).map(([k,v]) => `${esc(k)}: <span class="mono">${money(v)}</span>`).join(" &nbsp;·&nbsp; ")}

          <div style="margin-top:4px;font-weight:600;">Total charged this month: <span class="mono">${money(breakdown.total)}</span></div>

        </div>` : ""}

        <div class="row" style="margin-top:4px;"><button class="btnPrimary" data-action="recordPayment">${ICONS.plus} Record payment</button></div>

        <small class="hint">Selecting a student and month fills the amount and shows the fee breakdown by subject.</small>

      </div>

      ${sorted.length===0 ? `<p class="muted">No payments recorded yet.</p>` : `

        <table>

          <thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Student</th><th>Month</th><th>Method</th><th class="right">Amount</th><th>Receipt</th><th></th></tr></thead>

          <tbody>

            ${sorted.map((p) => `

              <tr>

                <td>${p.date}</td>

                <td>${esc(studentName(p.studentId))}</td>

                <td class="muted">${monthLabel(p.month)}</td>

                <td class="muted">${esc(p.method||"—")}</td>

                <td class="right mono">${money(p.amount)}</td>

                <td>${p.receiptData ? (p.receiptData.startsWith("data:image") ? `<a href="${p.receiptData}" target="_blank"><img class="receiptThumb" src="${p.receiptData}"/></a>` : `<a href="${p.receiptData}" target="_blank">${esc(p.receiptName||"View file")}</a>`) : `<span class="muted">—</span>`}</td>

                <td class="right"><button class="iconBtn" data-action="removePayment" data-id="${p.id}">${ICONS.trash}</button></td>

              </tr>`).join("")}

          </tbody>

        </table>

      `}

    </div>

    <div class="section">

      <div class="sectionHead"><div class="sectionTitle">${ICONS.wallet}<h2>Student Balances</h2></div></div>

      <p class="muted" style="margin-top:-6px;">Negative balance = credit (parent has prepaid ahead). Positive = still owed.</p>

      ${data.students.length===0 ? `<p class="muted">No students yet.</p>` : `

        <table>

          <thead><tr><th>Student</th><th class="right">Total charged</th><th class="right">Total paid</th><th class="right">Balance</th></tr></thead>

          <tbody>

            ${data.students.map((s) => {

              const charged = studentAllTimeCharged(s.id);

              const paid = studentAllTimePaid(s.id);

              const bal = charged - paid;

              const balDisplay = bal < 0 ? `-${money(Math.abs(bal))} (credit)` : (bal > 0 ? `${money(bal)} (due)` : money(0));

              return `<tr>

                <td>${esc(s.name)}</td>

                <td class="right mono">${money(charged)}</td>

                <td class="right mono">${money(paid)}</td>

                <td class="right mono ${bal<0?"balanceNeg":(bal>0?"balancePos":"")}">${balDisplay}</td>

              </tr>`;

            }).join("")}

          </tbody>

        </table>

      `}

    </div>

  `;

}


function renderReports() {

  return `

    <div class="section">

      <div class="sectionHead"><div class="sectionTitle">${ICONS.download}<h2>Reports &amp; Export</h2></div></div>

      <p class="muted" style="margin-top:-6px;">Export records for bookkeeping or Hong Kong Inland Revenue Department filing.</p>

      <div class="row">

        ${fieldHTML("From month", `<input type="month" id="repFrom" value="${ui.reportFrom}"/>`)}

        ${fieldHTML("To month", `<input type="month" id="repTo" value="${ui.reportTo}"/>`)}

      </div>

      <div class="row">

        <button class="btnPrimary" data-action="exportExcel">${ICONS.download} Export Excel (.xlsx)</button>

        <button class="btnPrimary" data-action="exportPDF">${ICONS.download} Export PDF (IRD report)</button>

      </div>

      <p class="muted" style="font-size:12px;">The Excel file includes a monthly summary plus full attendance, payment, room-rental and balance logs. The PDF is a formatted income &amp; expenditure summary with the centre's letterhead, suitable to present to IRD.</p>

    </div>

  `;

}


function render() {

  const app = document.getElementById("app");

  app.className = "";

  let body = "";

  if (ui.tab === "dashboard") body = renderDashboard();

  else if (ui.tab === "students") body = renderStudents();

  else if (ui.tab === "teachers") body = renderTeachers();

  else if (ui.tab === "lessonTypes") body = renderLessonTypes();

  else if (ui.tab === "attendance") body = renderAttendance();

  else if (ui.tab === "roomRental") body = renderRoomRental();

  else if (ui.tab === "payments") body = renderPayments();

  else body = renderReports();


  const banner = !storageOK ? `

    <div class="section" style="border-color:var(--burgundy);background:#fff3f0;">

      <div class="sectionTitle"><h2 style="color:var(--burgundy);font-size:15px;">⚠ Data isn't saving</h2></div>

      <p style="margin:6px 0 0;font-size:13px;">

        This page can't reach persistent storage right now${lastSaveError ? " (" + esc(lastSaveError) + ")" : ""}.

        This usually happens when the file is opened on its own outside the Claude conversation (e.g. downloaded and double-clicked, or opened in a plain browser tab) —

        saved-storage only works while this is viewed as the live artifact inside Claude. Keep working from the artifact panel in your Claude chat so nothing is lost.

      </p>

    </div>

  ` : "";


  app.innerHTML = `<div class="wrap">${renderHeader()}${banner}${body}</div>`;

  attachEvents();

}


function attachEvents() {

  document.querySelectorAll("[data-action]").forEach((el) => {

    const action = el.dataset.action;

    if (el.tagName === "TEXTAREA" && action === "editDueRemark") {

      el.addEventListener("change", (e) => updateRemark(el.dataset.id, e.target.value));

      return;

    }

    if (el.tagName === "SELECT" && action === "changeDueStatus") {

      el.addEventListener("change", (e) => changeDueStatus(el.dataset.id, e.target.value));

      return;

    }

    el.addEventListener("click", async () => {

      if (action === "setTab") { ui.tab = el.dataset.tab; render(); }

      else if (action === "setAttView") { ui.attView = el.dataset.view; render(); }


      else if (action === "editTeacher") editTeacher(el.dataset.id);

      else if (action === "cancelTeacherForm") cancelTeacherForm();

      else if (action === "saveTeacherForm") await saveTeacherForm();

      else if (action === "removeTeacher") await removeTeacher(el.dataset.id);


      else if (action === "editStudent") editStudent(el.dataset.id);

      else if (action === "cancelStudentForm") cancelStudentForm();

      else if (action === "saveStudentForm") await saveStudentForm();

      else if (action === "removeStudent") await removeStudent(el.dataset.id);

      else if (action === "addEnrollRow") addEnrollRow();

      else if (action === "removeEnrollRow") removeEnrollRow(Number(el.dataset.idx));


      else if (action === "editLessonType") editLessonType(el.dataset.id);

      else if (action === "cancelLessonTypeForm") cancelLessonTypeForm();

      else if (action === "saveLessonTypeForm") await saveLessonTypeForm();

      else if (action === "removeLessonType") await removeLessonType(el.dataset.id);


      else if (action === "pickDay") { ui.attSelectedDate = el.dataset.date; ui.attEditingId = null; render(); }

      else if (action === "closeDayPanel") { ui.attSelectedDate = null; ui.attEditingId = null; render(); }

      else if (action === "addSession") { ui.attEditingId = "new"; render(); }

      else if (action === "editSession") { ui.attEditingId = el.dataset.id; render(); }

      else if (action === "cancelSession") { ui.attEditingId = null; render(); }

      else if (action === "saveSession") await saveSessionFromForm(ui.attSelectedDate);

      else if (action === "deleteSession") await deleteAttendanceRecord(el.dataset.id);

      else if (action === "attMonthShift") {

        const [y,m] = ui.attMonth.split("-").map(Number);

        const nd = new Date(y, m-1+Number(el.dataset.dir), 1);

        ui.attMonth = `${nd.getFullYear()}-${String(nd.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}`;

        ui.attSelectedDate = null; ui.attEditingId = null;

        render();

      }

      else if (action === "toggleResolved") await toggleResolved(el.dataset.id);


      else if (action === "saveRoomRentalForm") await saveRoomRentalForm();

      else if (action === "editRoomRental") editRoomRental(el.dataset.id);

      else if (action === "cancelRoomRentalForm") cancelRoomRentalForm();

      else if (action === "removeRoomRental") await removeRoomRental(el.dataset.id);

      else if (action === "generateMonthLessons") await generateMonthLessons();


      else if (action === "recordPayment") {

        const fileInput = document.getElementById("payReceipt");

        let receiptData = null, receiptName = null;

        const file = fileInput && fileInput.files && fileInput.files[0];

        if (file) {

          if (file.size > 3 * 1024 * 1024) { alert("Receipt file is too large (max 3MB)."); return; }

          receiptData = await fileToBase64(file);

          receiptName = file.name;

        }

        await recordPayment(

          document.getElementById("payStudent").value,

          document.getElementById("payMonth").value,

          document.getElementById("payAmount").value,

          document.getElementById("payDate").value,

          document.getElementById("payMethod").value,

          receiptData, receiptName

        );

      }

      else if (action === "removePayment") await removePayment(el.dataset.id);


      else if (action === "exportExcel") exportExcel(document.getElementById("repFrom").value, document.getElementById("repTo").value);

      else if (action === "exportPDF") exportPDF(document.getElementById("repFrom").value, document.getElementById("repTo").value);

    });

  });


  const dashMonth = document.getElementById("dashMonth");

  if (dashMonth) dashMonth.addEventListener("change", (e) => { ui.dashMonth = e.target.value; render(); });


  const attStudentSel = document.getElementById("attStudentSel");

  if (attStudentSel) attStudentSel.addEventListener("change", (e) => {

    const val = e.target.value;

    setTimeout(() => { ui.attStudent = val; ui.attSelectedDate = null; ui.attEditingId = null; render(); }, 0);

  });


  const payStudent = document.getElementById("payStudent");

  const payMonth = document.getElementById("payMonth");

  const recalcSuggestedAmount = (e) => {

    const sid = document.getElementById("payStudent").value;

    const mo = document.getElementById("payMonth").value;

    // defer to next tick so the native <select>/<input> finishes its own

    // change handling before we replace the DOM via render()

    setTimeout(() => {

      ui.payStudentSel = sid; ui.payMonthSel = mo;

      if (sid && mo) ui.payAmount = studentMonthTotal(sid, mo).toFixed(2);

      render();

    }, 0);

  };

  if (payStudent) payStudent.addEventListener("change", recalcSuggestedAmount);

  if (payMonth) payMonth.addEventListener("change", recalcSuggestedAmount);

  const payAmount = document.getElementById("payAmount");

  if (payAmount) payAmount.addEventListener("input", (e) => { ui.payAmount = e.target.value; });


  const repFrom = document.getElementById("repFrom");

  if (repFrom) repFrom.addEventListener("change", (e) => { ui.reportFrom = e.target.value; });

  const repTo = document.getElementById("repTo");

  if (repTo) repTo.addEventListener("change", (e) => { ui.reportTo = e.target.value; });

}


(async function init() {

  await loadData();

  render();

})();

</script>

</body>

</html>
